- **taiwan-holiday**: Query Taiwan national holidays, working-day counts, and compensatory days via the taiwan-holiday-cli npm package (invoked over npx, no install needed). Use this skill whenever the user asks anything that combines Taiwan with dates — whether it is "is X a holiday", "how many working days in May", "when is the next long weekend", "next Chinese New Year", planning leave, or any schedule question that depends on the 中華民國政府行政機關辦公日曆表. Also use it for any date-math question the user anchors to Taiwan (e.g. "我下個月工作幾天", "10/10 是什麼日子", "接下來連假"), even when the user does not say the word "holiday" explic… ([lis186/taiwan-holiday-skills](https://github.com/lis186/taiwan-holiday-skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/taiwan-holiday) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **taiwan-holidays**: 查台灣政府行政機關辦公日曆表——國定假日、連假、補班日。放假嗎、補班嗎、下個連假、春節幾號、國慶連假、人事行政總處日曆的問題適用。免 API 金鑰、免登入。公司行號自訂行事曆、學校行事曆的細節不適用（學校另有規定）。 ([tahodev/baodao-skill](https://github.com/tahodev/baodao-skill/tree/HEAD/taiwan-holidays) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **taiwan-home-buying-guide**: 協助臺灣自住買方從入門、找房、實價比較、高 CP 值物件挖掘、看屋、社區與產權查核、房仲話術、出價議價、房貸預審與合法提高核貸成數，到簽約交屋及退出策略。當使用者提到臺灣買房、房屋網址或截圖、實價登錄、合理價、便宜房、CP值、房仲、斡旋、要約書、議價、銀行鑑價、房貸成數、自備款、新青安、看屋檢查、社區管理、漏水違建或買房流程時使用；必須先查核最新官方政策與資料。不要用於海外房產、純租屋、建商銷售文案、違法美化財力、保證核貸或取代銀行、估價師、地政士、律師與驗屋專業。 ([JustinChangTW/ai-skills-core](https://github.com/JustinChangTW/ai-skills-core/tree/HEAD/skills/08-finance-property/taiwan-home-buying-guide))
